thank you so much

**[00:16:21.880] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000181)  
Good afternoon po at maraming salamat po, Chair. Hello to our DPWH family.

**[00:16:28.860] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000182)  
last year po

**[00:16:30.000] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000183)  
usapan natin yung isang national comprehensive flood control and drainage plan that should cover

**[00:16:39.920] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000184)  
the entire nation pero sa naririnig ko at sa nakita kong presentation ng inyong opisina ay

**[00:16:46.500] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000185)  
parang ang impression ko puro local or regional palang ang mga plano natin may i know if could you

**[00:16:52.440] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000186)  
confirm if that is true that we still do not have a national plan for flood control and

**[00:17:00.000] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000187)  
drainage

**[00:17:02.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000188)  
Mr. President, you are correct. I'm sorry, Mr. Chairman, you are correct, Congressman Chel.

**[00:17:07.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000189)  
that the strategy of the department over the course of the last couple of years, or more than that even,

**[00:17:18.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000190)  
is to

**[00:17:19.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000191)  
have master plans per river per major river basin

**[00:17:24.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000192)  
So,

**[00:17:24.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000193)  
wala po talagang consolidated national

**[00:17:28.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000195)  
plan, but it has been done on a per river basin basis.

**[00:17:36.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000196)  
So, for example, the Cagayan Valley River Basin, the Pampanga River Basin, the Bicol River Basin, the Cebu River Basin, and so on.

**[00:17:47.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000197)  
The Maguindanao Central Mindanao River Basin.

**[00:17:51.690] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000198)  
And apparently, when I asked the same question, Mr. Chairman, ito daw po ang recommendation ng ating mga foreign partners to do it, major river basin and then also on a per

**[00:18:02.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000199)  
urban center basis especially for drainage master plan. So you are absolutely correct po. Wala po talagang national flood mitigation master plan.

**[00:18:13.260] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000200)  
So you are, are we as a, is the executive department abandoning the idea of

**[00:18:20.780] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000201)  
coming up with a national plan

**[00:18:23.120] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000202)  
and simply relying on the plans for major river basins as well as urban centers?

**[00:18:30.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000203)  
I don't think it's abandoning that plan, but I think based on the experts' advice, at least to the DPWH,

**[00:18:40.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000204)  
the best approach for the best strategic approach is really to do it on a per river basin

**[00:18:48.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000205)  
major river basin level however there are general

**[00:18:54.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000206)  
approaches, approaches which are standard to all the other major river basin master plans.

**[00:19:03.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000207)  
For example, the focus on upstream interventions rather than downstream interventions.

**[00:19:10.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000208)  
The focus on retarding basins rather than simply putting up dikes and revetments. And also, the focus on

**[00:19:22.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000209)  
the needs-based approach which essentially means that magtatayo lang po tayo ng mga dike sa mga area na talagang mayroon tayong pinoprotect ang mga populated areas.

**[00:19:35.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000210)  
uh i think there are general principles that are applicable nationwide however the specific

**[00:19:42.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000211)  
interventions according to the experts as they were explaining it to me need to be on a per

**[00:19:48.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000212)  
river basin, major river basin and per locality level.

**[00:19:54.560] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000213)  
Yeah, that's exactly why I'm asking those questions. Kasi kung wala po tayong national plan at hindi nakasaad doon kung ano

**[00:20:03.960] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000214)  
yung mga general principles that should apply anywhere in the country.

**[00:20:09.160] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000215)  
Kumbaga para rin tayong walang, by analogy, wala tayong national land use plan.

**[00:20:15.240] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000216)  
Mahirap naman, how can we

**[00:20:17.840] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000217)  
expect to develop rationally in the coming 5, 10 or 15 years without this kind of comprehensive approach?

**[00:20:27.480] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000218)  
Well, anyway, I think that's pretty clear.

**[00:20:29.680] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000219)

**[00:20:43.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000220)  
all the 18 major river basins uh mr chairman congressman shell have master plans

**[00:20:51.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000221)  
however

**[00:20:52.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000222)  
uh most of them i'm looking at the list right now we can provide for the list uh mr chairman

**[00:20:59.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000223)  
looking at it now all of them are actually being sorry out of the 18 14 are being updated

**[00:21:12.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000224)  
Mr. Chairman, because they were formulated years and years ago, outdated na po siya.

**[00:21:19.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000225)  
Yes po, yes po, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:21:21.360] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000225-split-1)  
And what is your target for making sure that all of the major river basins have updated plans?

**[00:21:29.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000226)  
Yeah, we are pushing po our consultants who are mostly foreign consultants to

**[00:21:35.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000227)  
complete all the updates by 2027. Hopefully, the first half of 2027, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:21:43.920] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000228)  
Kung ano po ang maitutulong namin to get that moving and to make sure that it is done as soon as possible, I really believe that

**[00:21:51.700] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000229)  
the more rational plans we have, the better for issues of flood control and drainage.

**[00:21:58.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000230)  
Mr. Chairman, if I may po, since nagtanong na rin po si Congressman Chel kung ano ang maitutulong, I think

**[00:22:04.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000231)  
a move in the right direction is really the proposed uh mind uh bill that is now i think

**[00:22:12.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000232)  
in Congress to have a national master plan.

**[00:22:16.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000233)  
But ang pakiusap lang din po namin, sana po pati flood mitigation isama din po doon. I think that is critical kasi nga po, tama po kayo eh. Kailangan po talaga meron tayong plano and unfortunately, yung dinatnan po natin eh medyo fragmented yung ating mga plano.

**[00:22:35.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000234)  
but i think the mind bill is definitely i hope that can pass

**[00:22:38.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000235)  
and we are fully fully supportive of that thank you mr chairman

**[00:22:42.640] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000236)  
Kaya ako po nire-raise yan, Sec. Vince ay just the other day, nakapunta po ako ng ilang bahagi ng Pampanga

**[00:22:50.580] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000237)  
where

**[00:22:51.640] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000238)  
flooding has not just been a recurring but appears to be a perennial problem

**[00:22:58.600] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000239)  
at the ducalang mismo na realize when i saw with my own eyes how everything comes to a standstill

**[00:23:04.860] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000240)  
when an area is flooded and the water doesn't go down

kanila mga ofisina mismo yung buong ekonomiya ay wala ng gagalaw and in the areas that i've been

**[00:23:27.600] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000244)

**[00:23:35.080] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000245)  
plano dahil pag um for administration lang ang ginagawa natin hindi natin malulutas itong mga

**[00:23:41.540] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000246)  
problemang ito i just want to emphasize that now moving on to another point ito naman ay question

**[00:23:48.520] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000247)  
ng pananagutan at pati transparency.

**[00:23:51.940] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000248)  
i'm very happy to hear about your portal we will be monitoring that to make sure that it's as

**[00:23:57.500] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000249)  
transparent as possible antanoko po is about lifestyle checks waivers and the conflict of

**[00:24:04.860] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000250)  
interest disclosures i think we had a similar discussion last year but may i know how regular

**[00:24:12.660] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000251)  
lifestyle checks are now being conducted in your department

**[00:24:18.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000252)  
Tuloy-tuloy po ang aming ano, ang aming

**[00:24:20.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000253)  
lifestyle checks

**[00:24:21.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000256)  
in fact we are working very very closely with the

**[00:24:25.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000257)  
office of the ombudsman on this uh

**[00:24:26.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000258)  
Ah, ano po ito no, tuloy-tuloy po tayo

**[00:24:29.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000259)  
And in fact, what we have done is we have really done a one-strike policy sa department, Mr. Chairman. Pag meron pong

**[00:24:37.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000260)  
reports and these are verified, not just by DPWH but also by our law enforcement and justice agencies

**[00:24:45.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000261)  
and justice agencies then immediately po ang mga tao po ay nag-i-issue po agad kami ng mga show cause

**[00:24:51.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000262)  
at in most cases like suspend and in some cases we have even terminated

**[00:24:57.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000263)  
people just based on reports on social media that have been properly verified and validated so

**[00:25:03.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000264)  
So tuloy-tuloy po tayo dyan. We are right now working din po on possible, on the possible

**[00:25:11.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000265)  
waivers and disclosures that can be done over and above the current requirements of the

**[00:25:17.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000266)  
statements of assets and liabilities etc and we are working for with our lawyers

**[00:25:24.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000267)  
and the other agencies

**[00:25:25.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000268)  
I also want to put of note, Mr. Chairman,

**[00:25:29.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000269)  
that on the part of contractors, private contractors,

**[00:25:35.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000270)  
we have recently started working with the Securities and Exchange Commission

**[00:25:40.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000271)  
through the office of Chairman Francis Lim.

**[00:25:43.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000272)  
And we are currently working together on a memorandum of agreement

**[00:25:47.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000273)  
to hold to a higher standard private contractors

**[00:25:53.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000274)  
who have contracts that involve public funds.

**[00:25:57.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000275)  
And these involve more stringent disclosure requirements,

**[00:26:06.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000276)

**[00:26:10.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000277)  
Dahil po their operations as private contractors

**[00:26:16.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000278)  
involve...

**[00:26:17.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000279)  
public funds and public interest.

**[00:26:20.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000280)  
So we will be happy to report to the committee

**[00:26:23.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000281)  
and to Congressman Chell on where that is going.

**[00:26:27.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000282)  
But we are hopeful that we can finalize that memorandum of agreement

**[00:26:31.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000283)  
which will be translated into both DPWH and SEC circulars

**[00:26:37.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000284)  
very soon, Mr. Chairman.

ongoing

**[00:28:53.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000326)  
which is mostly non-national roads

**[00:28:57.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000327)  
or local roads and bridges

**[00:28:59.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000328)  
and other infrastructure needed by communities.

**[00:29:02.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000329)  
But at the same time,

**[00:29:03.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000330)  
we have also made the requirements of these programs

**[00:29:06.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000331)  
more stringent as well.

**[00:29:08.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000332)  
To add to that, we have also...

**[00:29:11.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000333)  
streamlined

**[00:29:17.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000334)  
and removed the other portions

**[00:29:19.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000335)  
of the convergence program.

**[00:29:21.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000337)  
Specifically, the TICAS, the CATUPARAN,

**[00:29:25.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000338)  
the ROLL-IT, and TRIPSY.

**[00:29:29.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000339)  
Because we feel that the best departments to handle

**[00:29:34.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000340)  
and to identify the projects for these convergence programs

**[00:29:38.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000341)  
are the departments primarily in charge of those projects,

**[00:29:46.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000342)  
which are namely tourism for TRIPSY,

**[00:29:51.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000346)  
DTI for ROLL-IT,

**[00:29:52.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000347)  
DOTR for CATUPARAN,

**[00:29:54.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000348)  
and DND for TICA.

**[00:29:56.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000349)  
So, I think this is not only...

**[00:29:58.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000350)  
only avoids the potential use of these items for purposes other than what they were originally

**[00:30:09.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000351)  
envisioned for like roads to tourist destinations and roads to industrial parks but it also

**[00:30:18.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000352)  
ensures that the priority projects of those lead departments are the ones that are given the proper

**[00:30:26.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000353)  
funding so it's not perfect yet mr chairman but i think these uh reforms that we have instituted

**[00:30:33.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000354)  
for this are a step in the right direction.

**[00:30:37.000] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000354-split-1)  
Thank you so much. I have no more questions, Mr. Chair.

**[00:30:40.280] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000355)  
I would just like to suggest and I think this is already being considered by the department,

**[00:30:46.200] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000356)  
dun po sa mga plano for the major river basins and other planning,

**[00:30:50.720] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000357)  
that you also include the nature-based solutions to our issues thank you

**[00:30:57.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000358)  
If I may just quickly, Mr. Chairman, yung nature-based po talaga is really a critical component of what we are trying to do. As an example po, and the Vice Chair would know this very well.

**[00:31:11.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000359)  
For revetments po, just so we have an idea, yung mga dike po natin dati po kasi madalas, or hindi madalas, halos lagi ang dike po natin ay konkreto.

**[00:31:22.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000360)  
but as

**[00:31:24.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000361)  
As Undersecretary Lara has advised us when she was promoted as Undersecretary,

**[00:31:29.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000362)  
the recommendation typically po of experts is to use gabions instead of full concrete structures.

**[00:31:40.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000363)  
Primarily because they are more nature-based, but more so because concrete dikes po, they actually speed up the velocity of water which actually

**[00:31:54.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000364)  
actually causes more harm

**[00:31:55.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000365)  
they uh especially for

**[00:31:57.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000366)  
rural areas. That is why for rural areas, if you will notice po now,

**[00:32:03.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000367)  
when we repaired the structures, for example, in Cebu, in the Mananga River and the Butuanon Rivers,

**[00:32:08.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000368)  
kahit na po

**[00:32:10.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000369)  
concrete structures yung unang mga nilagay doon, yung pong

**[00:32:14.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000370)  
mga inayos

**[00:32:16.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000371)  
namin, gabion na po ang ginamit

**[00:32:18.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000372)  
namin, even for Davao.

**[00:32:19.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000373)  
Yun pong gabion na po ang ginagamit namin.

**[00:32:21.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000374)  
Ang mga concrete structures

**[00:32:24.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000375)  
lang po ginagamit mostly sa mga urban

**[00:32:26.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000376)  
areas tulad sa Manggahan,

**[00:32:27.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000377)  
sa Marikina or sa Pasig.

**[00:32:29.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000378)  
But I think these are just clear

**[00:32:32.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000379)  
examples of how effective these nature-based solutions are

**[00:32:35.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000380)  
because not only are they effective but they are also cheaper.

**[00:32:39.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000381)  
So we will report on that also as to how many we are already doing as of the moment and how many more we are doing in the coming years.

**[00:32:48.800] Rep. Jose Manuel Tadeo "Chel" I. Diokno** (asr-000382)  
Thank you. Thank you so much. Thank you, Chair Momo.

**[00:32:51.280] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000383)  
Thank you, Cong Chel. Thank you, Secretary. In addition to the comment of Secretary,

**[00:32:56.980] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000384)  
I am really in agreement with the Secretary that we really have to pause the MIND bill

**[00:33:02.180] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000385)  
MIND bill is now being deliberated in my committee and i am the principal author of the MIND bill

**[00:33:08.480] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000386)  
however in the MIND bill i would like to inform everyone

**[00:33:11.940] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000387)  
that there is really it's quite impossible for us to come up with a national

**[00:33:18.220] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000388)  
um,

**[00:33:19.320] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000389)  
master plan of flood control. The reason why, because we are island, we are composed of islands.

**[00:33:26.540] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000390)  
and therefore the characteristic one of one river basin one river basin for example in luzon

**[00:33:31.120] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000391)  
could not be connected with the river basin in visayas and could that become more much more in

**[00:33:35.660] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000392)  
in mindanao just the reason why we cannot come up with the national master plan however we are

**[00:33:41.960] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000393)  
However, we are in agreement with the consultants that

**[00:33:46.020] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000394)  
master plan should be by river basin. And at the same time, tama si

**[00:33:56.560] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000395)  
Usec. Lara na siguro yung

**[00:34:01.000] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000396)  
nature-based na gamit natin.

**[00:34:03.760] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000397)  
gamit natin the reason probably of using um gabion because it is not rigid structure that's the

**[00:34:11.720] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000398)  
reason why that's why kung uh rigid structure kasi katulad ng concrete masisira babagsak talaga yan

**[00:34:17.940] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000399)  
pero gabions, by weight, bababa lang, bababa yan. I even suggest the use also of geotubes, if you can use it, geotubes.

**[00:34:28.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000400)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:34:29.860] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000400-split-1)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[00:34:30.580] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-000401)  
Next to interpellate is from the majority, Honorable Shirlyn Bañas-Nograles.

**[00:34:41.180] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000402)  
good afternoon sec sec i have uh three concerns one is on the detailed engineering design

**[00:34:51.920] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000403)  
of tpwh of course every year we have thousands of projects

**[00:34:58.070] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000404)  
as a matter of practice how much do you allocate for the detailed engineering design and of course

**[00:35:04.500] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000405)  
that is during the preparatory stage that includes the geotechnical studies

**[00:35:09.640] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000406)  
hydrologic mapping and so on and so forth because i've observed as observed uh

**[00:35:16.760] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000407)  
sec as observed

**[00:35:21.740] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000408)  
um in the menu of projects of tpwh this is one among this among the weak points in tpwh

**[00:35:31.000] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000409)  
uh to cite a few

**[00:35:36.180] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000410)  
underpass in general santo city

**[00:35:40.740] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000411)  
you making problema po is the highly inaccurate geotechnical studies

**[00:35:47.080] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000412)  
geotechnical investigations and of course

**[00:35:51.860] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000413)  
mali po sa umpisa, mali na po yung design.

**[00:35:54.880] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000414)  
Kaya po ang nangyari, instead na dalawang taon lang yung project,

**[00:35:59.380] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000415)  
umabot ng apat na taon.

**[00:36:01.580] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000416)  
And because of that, a lot of establishments has really suffered.

**[00:36:07.080] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000417)  
In fact, may mga na-bankrupt, ang dami pong nawala ng trabaho.

**[00:36:11.500] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000418)  
So, hindi lang po sa Jensa nangyayari yan.

**[00:36:15.440] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000419)  
In my research, I don't know if my research is accurate,

**[00:36:21.980] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000420)  
parang nangyari din po yan sa flyover, doon po sa unka, unka flyover.

**[00:36:28.480] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000421)  
It turned out na highly inaccurate din yung geotechnical studies.

**[00:36:34.540] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000422)  
Kaya malambot ang lupa, kaya nag-sink po yung flyover.

**[00:36:39.860] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000423)  
And hence, of course, causing millions of losses from the end of the government.

**[00:36:47.260] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000424)  
And at the same time po, perwisyo sa stakeholders natin.

**[00:36:51.380] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000425)  
In the recent earthquake also in General Santo City,

**[00:36:55.740] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000426)  
I've noticed na hindi po naging problema yung structure.

**[00:37:00.680] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000427)  
Although there are exceptions, may mga konteng substandard na structures observed.

**[00:37:06.460] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000428)  
But generally, yung nakita din po natin na mga problema is that

**[00:37:11.860] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000429)  
the lack of geotechnical studies prior to the construction.

**[00:37:17.200] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000430)  
Ang dami pong mga structure na actually hindi problema,

**[00:37:21.780] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000431)  
during the earthquake, structurally, hindi po problematic,

**[00:37:26.500] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000432)  
but ang naging problema po is the soil liquefaction.

**[00:37:29.640] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000433)  
These problems could have been prevented

**[00:37:33.580] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000434)  
pag nagkaroon po ng proper na geotechnical studies.

**[00:37:38.220] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000435)  
And then, with all of these things going on,

**[00:37:43.920] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000436)  
as secretary, ano po ang plano ninyo dito?

**[00:37:47.720] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000437)  
Are we going to continuously outsource this?

**[00:37:51.840] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000438)  
Kasi ang nanotice ko po, if we contract consultant during the preparatory stage,

**[00:37:57.300] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000439)  
if something goes wrong during the construction, ano yung magiging accountability nila?

**[00:38:03.240] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000440)  
Because the contract ends during the preparatory stage.

**[00:38:09.740] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000441)  
And, sige Sec, sige okay.

**[00:38:12.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000442)  
Actually, I agree with you 100%.

**[00:38:15.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000443)  
I will just give some data, ma'am, if you don't mind.

**[00:38:20.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000444)  
Mr. Chairman, Congresswoman Shergin is absolutely correct.

**[00:38:24.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000445)  
Critical po ang feasibility studies and detailed engineering kasama na po ang geotech.

**[00:38:31.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000446)  
Napakalaki po problema ng DPWH.

**[00:38:34.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000447)  
Ngayon, lalong-lalo na sa mga mountainous regions,

**[00:38:42.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000448)  
dahil po ang ating mga kalye e bumabagsak

**[00:38:47.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000449)  
at ang main reason po na bumabagsak is kulang po at mali,

**[00:38:53.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000450)  
in some cases, mali, ang geotech studies.

**[00:38:56.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000451)  
Totoo, totoo po yan.

**[00:38:57.780] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000452)  
Follow-up question ko po, sige.

**[00:38:59.740] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000453)  
In the many instances that happened in the many infrastructure

**[00:39:04.840] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000454)  
that is being implemented by DPWH,

**[00:39:09.360] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000455)  
yung mga consultants po na hinire natin na gumawa nito mga geotechnical studies po

**[00:39:14.700] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000456)  
and investigations,

**[00:39:16.480] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000457)  
meron po ba kayong any of the consultants that was held accountable or liable?

**[00:39:22.120] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000458)  
Kasi,

**[00:39:23.220] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000459)  
the project has caused losses to the government.

**[00:39:25.380] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000460)  
Millions of losses to the government.

**[00:39:27.520] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000461)  
And also,

**[00:39:29.400] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000462)  
problems, of course, in the locality.

**[00:39:31.980] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000463)  
So, meron po ba kayong na-prosecute?

**[00:39:34.300] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000464)  
Meron po ba kayong na-blacklist?

**[00:39:36.080] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000465)  
Sa pagkadami-dami po na mga nangyari dito sa Pilipinas na ganong sitwasyon?

**[00:39:41.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000466)  
Ma'am, ako po ay nandito pa lang nang wala pang isang taon.

**[00:39:46.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000467)  
So, I'm asking them in the past year, mukhang wala.

**[00:39:49.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000468)  
But, let me find out po kung meron ng na-

**[00:39:51.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000469)  
na-hold-accountable na geotech company in the past.

**[00:39:56.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000470)  
But, I think what's important po is,

**[00:39:58.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000471)  
I just consulted po with our technical people that ideally po,

**[00:40:04.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000472)  
and the vice-chair would agree with this,

**[00:40:06.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000473)  
dapat po 5 to 6 percent ng total project cost, meaning ng capital outlay ng DPWH,

**[00:40:12.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000474)  
dapat po is devoted to feasibility studies, detailed engineering, geotech, etc.

**[00:40:19.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000475)  
So, if we look at po the 2026 budget,

**[00:40:22.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000476)  
of 460 billion in capital outlay,

**[00:40:26.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000477)  
5 to 6 percent of 460 is roughly about 30 billion pesos.

**[00:40:33.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000478)  
Ang budget lang po for 2026 for these studies is only 6 billion.

**[00:40:40.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000479)  
30 billion dapat, pero 6 billion lang po.

**[00:40:44.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000480)  
So, ang ibig pong sabihin ito is, all these projects,

**[00:40:48.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000481)  
I think we have about 20,000, 17,000, 18,000 projects,

**[00:40:52.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000482)  
in 2026, all those are competing for a fraction of the required amount

**[00:41:00.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000483)  
to do proper FS, PDE, and other geotech studies.

**[00:41:06.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000484)  
So, ang nangyayari po dito dahil kurang ang pondo,

**[00:41:08.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000485)  
either ang ginawa po namin,

**[00:41:12.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000486)  
nagimbawa po nung 2025, nagkaroon po tayo ng savings,

**[00:41:16.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000487)  
nagpa-augment po kami ng PDE at mga,

**[00:41:20.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000488)  
I think, may nagpa-augment po kami ng parang 1 billion.

**[00:41:24.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000489)  
Kasi marami pong mga nasirang mga roads na kailangan ng geotech,

**[00:41:31.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000490)  
katulad nung Buda Road, na huwag ang pagkukuha na ng pondo.

**[00:41:35.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000491)  
So, it's really a problem, Madam Congresswoman and Chair.

**[00:41:42.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000492)  
And I would really request po na sana, huwag na pong mabawasan sana

**[00:41:47.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000493)  
ang PDE and FS.

**[00:41:51.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000494)  
Kasi nagsa-suffer po taga ang mga studies dahil sa kakagangan din sa pondo.

**[00:41:57.440] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000495)  
It's like I'm not expert in the field, no?

**[00:42:33.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000504)

**[00:42:53.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000507)

**[00:42:59.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000508)

**[00:43:02.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-000509)

**[00:43:10.660] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-000510)

**[01:29:51.660] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001889)  
Now, to my question.

**[01:29:55.000] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001891)  
Mr. Chairman, my question this afternoon is directed to the Secretary of Public Works and Highways, and if the DBM is present, this is also addressed to the DBM.

**[01:30:09.340] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001892)  
I will confine my interpellation to a single item.

**[01:30:13.540] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001893)  
Special Provision No. 10 of the DPWH 2027 NEP, Page 7, Volume 2, does be on the disposition of the Motor Vehicles Users Charge Collections.

**[01:30:28.200] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001894)  
My concern is not the amount, it is the presentation.

**[01:30:32.220] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001895)  
And what that presentation does is statutorily earmarked special fund.

**[01:30:43.800] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001896)  
Mr. Secretary, in the fiscal year 2026, and even in the previous years, the special road fund appeared as a stand-alone PAP or program activity and project.

**[01:30:57.580] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001897)  
Under the DPWH Convergence and Special Support Program, entitled Special Road Fund Motor Vehicles User Charge with a peso amount of 14, ito yung 2026,

**[01:31:11.750] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001898)  
14,833,952,000.

**[01:31:16.420] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001899)  
In the fiscal year 2027 NEP, the same line item, a stand-alone PAP, is no longer there.

**[01:31:24.320] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001900)  
The fiscal year 2026 GAA Special Provision was titled Special Road Fund.

**[01:31:30.140] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001901)  
The fiscal year 2027-NEP Special Provision No. 10 is now titled Disposition of the Motor Vehicles Users Charge Collection.

**[01:31:40.220] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001902)  
The title of the provision was changed.

**[01:31:47.260] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001903)  
And the 15 billion that used to be a stand-alone Special Road Fund is now, for Special Provision No. 10, is specifically included under the Asset Preservation Program.

**[01:32:02.240] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001904)  
So on the face of the fiscal year 2027-NEP, in the PAP schedule that this committee is being asked to approve, a legislator cannot see the Special Road Fund as a separate line.

**[01:32:16.820] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001907)  
It is invisible unless one reads Special Provision No. 10 and knows how to look for it inside the Asset Preservation Program.

**[01:32:28.000] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001908)  
Am I correct, Mr. Secretary?

**[01:32:31.280] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001909)  
And may I add just a question, did the DPWH requested this provision or was it imposed upon you by the DBM?

**[01:32:51.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001910)  
Mr. Chairman, if I may, you know,

**[01:32:56.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001911)  
This is, I think, a special provision for this year.

**[01:33:01.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-001912)  
May I ask our Undersecretary for planning, with the indulgence of our Honorable Congressman from Davao City, Undersecretary Nick Conte, to respond.

**[01:33:13.040] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001913)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, and maraming salamat po, Congressman, for that question.

**[01:33:17.980] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001914)  
Tama po kayo. In the previous years, itemized po yung MVUC.

**[01:33:21.840] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001915)  
But for this year, following yung amendment,

**[01:33:25.700] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001916)  
But for this year, following yung amendment nga po ng Republic Act 8794 by Republic Act 11239,

**[01:33:31.080] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001917)  
kung saan na-limit na po yung gamit ng MVUC dito po sa Construction, Upgrading, Repair, and Rehabilitation of Roads, Bridges, and Road Drainage.

**[01:33:42.460] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001918)  
So, ang ginawa po dito, by attribution, meaning there are projects in the Asset Under Organizational Outcome No. 1

**[01:33:50.760] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001919)  
on Asset Preservation for Construction, Upgrading, Repair, and Rehabilitation of Roads, Bridges, and Road Drainage.

**[01:33:57.760] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001920)  
which are actually attributed po yung magiging source po, Mr. Chairman, Honorable Congressman,

**[01:34:05.960] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001921)  
galing po doon sa MBUC, which are collections that should be remitted to the Treasury under a special account in the General Fund.

**[01:34:13.360] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001922)  
As a matter of fact, meron naman po, Mr. Chairman, which we can furnish the committee,

**[01:34:17.580] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001923)  
kung saan po naka-attribute yung pondo po na yun under Organizational Outcome No. 1 to address the issue of visibility,

**[01:34:25.400] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001924)  
hindi nyo nakikita.

**[01:34:26.200] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001925)  
we have and we can furnish po the committee of the list of the projects under, again, Asset Preservation Organizational Outcome No. 1

**[01:34:35.160] DPWH Undersecretary Nicasio A. Conti** (asr-001926)  
amounting to the amount which was specified by the DBM, amounting to P15 billion for the year 2027 po, Mr. Chairman, Honorable Congressman.

**[01:34:45.860] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001927)  
Let me continue my question on Special Provision No. 10.

**[01:34:50.640] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001928)  
Mr. Secretary, would you agree that the Special Road Fund is a special fund?

**[01:34:54.720] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001929)  
Within the meaning of Article 6, Section 29, Paragraph 3 of the Constitution,

**[01:35:00.640] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001930)  
there is money collected on tax levied for a special purpose to be treated as a special fund and paid out for such purpose only.

**[01:35:08.580] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001931)  
And the Asset Preservation Program, on the other hand, is a regular DPWH program funded.

**[01:35:18.597] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001932)  
Your Honor, I think this is a very important question

**[01:35:21.652] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001933)  
Please proceed, but that will be the last

**[01:35:24.301] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001934)  
A regular DPWH program funded from the general appropriation, not from earmarked revenues

**[01:35:29.120] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001935)  
So, we are talking about two different kinds of fund.

**[01:35:32.600] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001936)  
One that is a statutorily earmarked special fund with appropriations under PD 1234.

**[01:35:39.100] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001937)  
The other is an ordinary annual appropriation whose amount and scope are set by the DBM and by Congress each year.

**[01:35:45.640] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001938)  
Under Republic Act 8794 as amended by RA 11239, the MVUC earmarked funds cover construction,

**[01:35:53.900] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001939)  
upgrading, repair, and rehabilitation of roads, bridges, and road drainage.

**[01:35:58.600] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001940)  
Under Fiscal Year 2027, the Asset Preservation covers preventive maintenance and rehabilitation of roads and drainage.

**[01:36:06.420] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001941)  
But bridges are in a separate PAP, the bridge program.

**[01:36:10.580] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001942)  
So, when the MVUC is subsumed inside Asset Preservation, part of the statutorily scope, the earmarked bridges, is no longer within the PAP.

**[01:36:22.480] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001943)  
That carries.

**[01:36:23.760] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001944)  
The MVUC amount.

**[01:36:25.060] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001945)  
Is that not a narrowing of the operative use of the statutorily earmarked?

**[01:36:29.840] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001946)  
So, deritsuhin ko na lang. Taposin ko na lang yung question ko.

**[01:36:33.140] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001947)  
Now, Special Provision No. 10 requires the DPWH to disaggregate the Asset Preservation Program

**[01:36:41.300] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001948)  
and submit to Congress the list of regular versus MVUC funded Asset Preservation Projects within 15 days

**[01:36:49.880] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001949)  
from the effectivity of the GAA.

**[01:36:54.860] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001950)  
That report is submitted after the GAA is approved and takes effect.

**[01:37:00.240] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001951)  
It cannot inform this committee's deliberations on the 2027 appropriation because it does not yet exist when we vote.

**[01:37:09.320] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001952)  
Instead, the Special Road Fund were carried away as a separate PAP line on the face of the enacted GAAH as it was in the 2024, 2025, and 2026.

**[01:37:21.160] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001953)  
Then, the 15 billion MVUC amount would be visible to this committee.

**[01:37:25.200] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001954)  
At that time, the amount would be visible to the committee at the time of enactment and, of course, during deliberation.

**[01:37:35.560] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001955)  
And the 15-day post-effectivity disaggregation would not be necessary at all because the fund would already be separately visible in the GAA.

**[01:37:45.100] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001956)  
how we retained the previous stand-alone PAP for Special Road Fund.

**[01:37:54.000] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001957)  
So, the 15-day disaggregation, again, and the 15-day post-effectivity disaggregation report would not be necessary at all

**[01:38:03.400] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001958)  
because it would be already separately visible in the GAA, that is, if we follow the same 2026 and previous years.

**[01:38:11.580] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001959)  
So, the 15-day disaggregation requirement in the Special Provision No. 10, in effect, is a workaround for a problem later on.

**[01:38:18.740] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001960)  
It's self-creating by removing the stand-alone PAP.

**[01:38:21.860] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001962)  
Let me emphasize that post-enactment project identification has already been struck down by the Supreme Court.

**[01:38:31.300] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001963)  
Last 10 seconds.

**[01:38:36.520] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001964)  
Lastly Mr. Chairman, I would like to inform the Honorable Secretary and this body that there is a pending case right now being heard in the Supreme Court

**[01:38:51.180] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001965)  
challenging the constitutionality of the No. 1 unprogrammed funds.

**[01:38:57.140] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001966)  
This case is a case of automatic appropriations, earmark funds, such as the SAGF, special accounts in the general fund,

**[01:39:06.020] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001967)  
and the UIGF, use of income in the general fund, which includes the Special Road Fund.

**[01:39:15.140] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001968)  
And the Supreme Court has already heard oral arguments five times.

**[01:39:21.180] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001969)  
So, I think everybody of us here knows.

**[01:39:25.520] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001970)  
That there are several cases already ruled that a General Appropriations Act, which is a general law, cannot amend a special law, which is the MVUC and its amended version.

**[01:39:41.680] Rep. Isidro T. Ungab** (asr-001971)  
So, your reply?

**[01:39:42.860] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-001972)

**[02:16:35.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002991)

**[02:16:42.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002992)  
Regions. Pero may mga iba-ibang exceptions. Kasi yan na raw ang kinagawian.

**[02:16:48.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002993)  
Okay. Ang ipinopose natin dito, ano yung kinagawian na yun?

**[02:16:52.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002994)  
Well, among others, nakita natin sa flood control, yung nasa central, nasa region, sabi niyan, indication, ang isang issue dyan, sino ang may-ari ng proyekto o kontrata?

**[02:17:06.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002995)

**[02:17:13.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002996)

**[02:17:22.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-002997)

**[02:17:26.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002998)

**[02:17:27.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002998-split-1)

**[02:17:41.980] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003000)  
Next.

**[02:17:42.500] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-003001)  
turn to interpelate is from the majority, Congressman Milagros Aquino. Congressman Mark Cojuangco.

**[02:18:05.080] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003003)

**[02:18:25.240] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003004)

**[02:18:34.920] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003005)

**[02:18:50.060] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003006)

**[02:18:59.700] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003007)

**[02:19:15.620] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003008)

**[02:19:24.640] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003009)

**[02:19:34.620] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003010)

**[02:19:42.200] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003011)

**[02:19:47.300] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003012)  
Sa public perception, pwede pong

**[02:19:51.620] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003013)  
ang right-of-way purchase

**[02:19:53.680] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003014)  
ay lumabas na cleanest form

**[02:19:55.900] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003015)  
or purest form

**[02:19:58.780] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003016)  
of public spending.

**[02:19:59.940] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003017)  
kasi wala pong hao-siao

**[02:20:02.920] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003018)  
sa pagbili ng right of way.

**[02:20:07.640] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003019)

**[02:20:17.680] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003020)

**[02:20:27.320] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003021)

**[02:20:35.600] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003022)

**[02:20:43.460] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003023)

**[02:20:47.260] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003024)  
Sa palagay ko po, dapat ang isang kongresista ay may karapatan magmungkahi na kung gusto niyang mabigyan

**[02:21:00.900] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003025)  
ang barangay sa distrito niya ng pondo para sa pagbili ng open space, maaari pong mangyari yun.

**[02:21:31.080] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003028)  
Yun lang po ang aking pahayag ngayong hapon.

**[02:21:36.460] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003029)

**[02:21:47.000] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-003030)

**[02:23:02.020] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003048)

**[02:23:17.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003051)

**[02:23:22.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003052)

**[02:23:32.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003053)

**[02:23:40.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003054)

**[02:23:42.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003055)

**[02:23:45.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003056)

**[02:23:54.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003057)  
Ikagawa po yan ay madalas hindi kino-coordinate sa mga local governments.

**[02:24:00.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003058)

**[02:24:07.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003059)

**[02:24:23.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003061)

**[02:24:30.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003062)

**[02:24:38.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003063)

**[02:24:44.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003064)

**[02:24:49.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003065)

**[02:24:54.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003066)

**[02:24:58.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003067)

**[02:25:08.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003068)

**[02:25:23.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003071)

First time po yan.

**[02:25:50.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003076)

foolproof?

**[02:25:54.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003078)

**[02:25:56.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-003081)
