**[03:16:54.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004055)  
that will correct the long-standing issues in the department.

**[03:16:59.260] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004056)  
Which I also agree with the good Secretary.

**[03:17:02.360] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004057)  
In fairness, malayo na rin in terms of sa planning ngayon,

**[03:17:06.240] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004058)  
halos nakikita na talaga natin

**[03:17:08.740] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004059)  
na shovel-ready yung mga project na nasasubmit.

**[03:17:11.820] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004060)  
In fairness, Secretary.

**[03:17:13.080] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004062)  
Yan ang isa ding magandang nagbago sa nakita ko for 2027.

**[03:17:17.960] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004063)  
Preparation.

**[03:17:20.280] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004064)  
But lastly, Secretary,

**[03:17:22.420] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004065)  
again, nagpapasalamat ako sa tulong mo

**[03:17:26.800] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004066)  
for the rehabilitation of the highway.

**[03:17:28.460] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004067)  
And siguro last na tanong ko na lang,

**[03:17:32.940] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004068)  
papano kayo natin na hindi na tayo maulit sa ganitong problema?

**[03:17:36.300] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004069)  
Kasi took us four years before na maayos talaga yung highway.

**[03:17:40.760] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004070)  
Every budget hearing, nandito kami,

**[03:17:44.420] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004071)  
paulit-ulit yung sinasabi namin,

**[03:17:45.660] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004072)  
halos siguro memorize na kami ng mga taga-dipi na,

**[03:17:49.800] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004073)  
so papano kaya na as we move forward,

**[03:17:52.480] Rep. Stephen James T. Tan** (asr-004074)  
hindi na siguro maulit pa yung ganitong experience?

**[03:17:58.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004075)  
Unang-una po, Mr. Chairman,

**[03:18:00.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004076)  
kasama ko po si Kong Stephen at si Kong Mike,

**[03:18:03.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004077)  
kasama po si Gov Shari,

**[03:18:05.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004078)  
inikot po namin ang buong Marica Highway ng Samar,

**[03:18:08.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004079)  
kasama na rin po ang Northern Samar.

**[03:18:10.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004080)  
At talaga pong grabe po talaga ang sitwasyon doon.

**[03:18:14.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004081)  
Pero tingin ko po, unang-una,

**[03:18:17.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004082)  
dalawang bagay.

**[03:18:18.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004083)  
Unang-una, kailangan ma-plano nang maigi.

**[03:18:21.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004084)  
Nandito po si Congresswoman Deth Escudero.

**[03:18:25.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004085)  
Kung titignan po natin ang Marica Highway segment ng Sorsogon,

**[03:18:28.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004086)  
napakaayos po.

**[03:18:30.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004087)  
Dinaanan ko din po yan.

**[03:18:32.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004088)  
At yan po, talagang pinag-plano ng tama,

**[03:18:35.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004089)  
kinonstruct ng tama.

**[03:18:36.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004090)  
So tingin ko po, unang-una, kailangan planuhin ng tama.

**[03:18:39.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004091)  
Dahil marami pong challenges sa area ng Samar

**[03:18:42.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004092)  
at sa area ng ibang areas po ng Bicol.

**[03:18:44.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004093)  
Ngayon po, talagang dinetalye na po natin

**[03:18:47.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004094)  
yung engineering planning.

**[03:18:48.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004095)  
Nito, humingi pa po tayo ng tulong

**[03:18:51.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004096)  
sa ating mga Japanese consultants

**[03:18:53.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004097)  
para po po rin yung mga problem areas

**[03:18:55.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004098)  
sa Samar at sa Andaya Highway

**[03:18:57.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004099)  
na laging tinutubig at nasisira ang ilalim

**[03:19:00.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004100)  
e ma-address na.

**[03:19:02.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004101)  
Kaya tingin ko po ngayon na nag-implement na tayo

**[03:19:05.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004102)  
both sa Andaya at sa parts of Albay

**[03:19:09.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004103)  
and also sa Samar and Northern Samar

**[03:19:12.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004104)  
e maayos na po yung disenyo nitong mga to.

**[03:19:15.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004105)  
Meron na po tayong proper cross-drainages,

**[03:19:18.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004106)  
gumagamit na po tayo ng bagong technology

**[03:19:20.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004107)  
such as geotextile technology na re-recommenda po ng Japan.

**[03:19:26.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004108)  
So masasolve na po yun.

**[03:19:28.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004109)  
Ikalawa po, kailangan po tuloy-tuloy ang pagpupondo.

**[03:19:33.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004110)  
At nagpapasalamat po ako sa Stephen,

**[03:19:38.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004111)  
sa kaya kong Mike at sa mga congressman po natin

**[03:19:42.170] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004112)  
at mga congressman natin sa Bicol at sa iba pang area

**[03:19:46.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004113)  
na sirasira ang Maharlika Highway.

**[03:19:48.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004114)  
Dahil po talagang sinusuporta nila tayo dyan.

**[03:19:51.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004115)  
And tingin ko po at ito po ay kinumit ng Pangulo

**[03:19:54.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004116)  
na before the end of his term,

**[03:19:56.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004117)  
at least yung mga perennial na sirasirang parte

**[03:20:00.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004118)  
ng Maharlika Highway sa Region 4A,

**[03:20:03.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004119)  
sa southern parts of Quezon,

**[03:20:06.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004120)  
mula Lopez, sorry, mula Pagbilaw hanggang Tagkawayan,

**[03:20:10.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004121)  
sa Andaya Highway po, sa CamSur,

**[03:20:13.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004122)  
some parts of Albay,

**[03:20:17.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004123)  
isiskip ko po.

**[03:20:17.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004124)  
Ang sorsugon, dahil maayos po ang sorsugon.

**[03:20:20.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004125)  
Tapos pagpasok na po ng Northern Samar hanggang Tacloban,

**[03:20:24.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004126)  
e tingin ko po, e maayos na po natin.

**[03:20:28.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004127)  
At slowly but surely po, nakikita naman po natin

**[03:20:30.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004128)  
sa tulong ng ating mga congressman,

**[03:20:34.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004129)  
yung mga iba pong area na dinaanan natin,

**[03:20:36.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004130)  
kung yung Pinabacdao, yung ibang area sa 1st District din,

**[03:20:40.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004131)  
dahan-dahan pong naayos na po natin with your help.

**[03:20:43.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004132)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[03:20:44.180] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004133)  
Thank you, congressman James.

**[03:20:46.820] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004134)  
And thank you.

**[03:20:47.800] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004135)  
Secretary.

**[03:20:48.580] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004136)  
Next, from the majority,

**[03:20:50.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004137)  
Congressman Howard Guintu.

**[03:20:55.000] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004138)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:20:56.860] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004139)  
Mr. Chair, I would like to ask,

**[03:20:58.940] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004140)  
regarding the policy of DPWH

**[03:21:00.940] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004141)  
in allocating funds for right-of-way acquisition and compensation,

**[03:21:06.680] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004142)  
and regarding that, what is the process?

**[03:21:10.000] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004143)  
Is it centralized or separated for projects?

**[03:21:21.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004144)  
Very sorry po, Cong Howard.

**[03:21:23.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004145)  
I'm sorry.

**[03:21:23.760] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004146)  
No problem.

**[03:21:25.380] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004147)  
Mr. Chair, I'd like to ask lang po,

**[03:21:27.520] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004148)  
regarding po yung sa allocating ng funds for right-of-way acquisitions

**[03:21:30.920] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004149)  
and yung compensation po,

**[03:21:32.960] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004150)  
yun po ba ay hiwalay sa pagpo-procure natin ng project

**[03:21:36.120] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004151)  
or yung paglalaan po ng pondo,

**[03:21:38.280] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004152)  
papaano po ba yung proseso nun sa right-of-way?

**[03:21:42.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004153)  
Hiwalay po sa project, Mr. Chairman,

**[03:21:46.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004154)  
ang region po ang nagpo-proseso.

**[03:21:49.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004155)  
Ngayon po ay, ano po, no,

**[03:21:51.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004156)  
pinaprioritize po natin ngayon

**[03:21:54.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004157)  
yung mga payment-ready na tinatawag

**[03:21:57.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004158)  
o voucher-ready na tinatawag.

**[03:21:59.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004159)  
natin.

**[03:21:59.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004160)  
either may court order na

**[03:22:01.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004161)  
or negotiated na

**[03:22:04.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004162)  
at pumayag na po ang mga nagbenta ng lupa

**[03:22:08.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004163)  
at yung mga expropriation cases po

**[03:22:12.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004164)  
na may order na po na mag-deposit ng 15%

**[03:22:17.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004165)  
sa korte nung ating right-of-way payments.

**[03:22:23.920] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004166)  
Mr. Chair,

**[03:22:24.820] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004167)  
based po dun sa ating nirequest,

**[03:22:28.940] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004168)  
pwede po ba man natin malaman kung ilan,

**[03:22:30.580] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004169)  
how much po yung inallocate natin

**[03:22:32.800] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004170)  
for fiscal year 2027?

**[03:22:38.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004171)  
15 billion po ang hiningi namin ngayong 2027.

**[03:22:44.300] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004172)  
15 billion po yung hiningi.

**[03:22:46.040] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004173)  
Sapat na po ba yun para sa...

**[03:22:48.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004174)  
Hindi po.

**[03:22:49.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004176)  
Halos 90 billion po ang utang.

**[03:22:53.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004177)  
Pero in the past po,

**[03:22:54.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004178)  
last year po,

**[03:22:55.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004179)  
this year po, 5 billion lang po.

**[03:22:59.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004180)  
5 billion.

**[03:23:00.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004181)  
Ngayon pong 2026 po ang mabibigay.

**[03:23:02.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004182)  
Madalas po talagang napag-iiwanan po

**[03:23:05.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004183)  
ang right-of-way payments.

**[03:23:07.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004184)  
Kaya hihingi po talaga kami ng tulong.

**[03:23:10.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004185)  
Palaki po nang palaki ang utang natin

**[03:23:12.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004186)  
sa ating mga kapabayan.

**[03:23:15.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004187)  
At dumadagdag po,

**[03:23:16.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004188)  
lalo na yung mga court-ordered

**[03:23:18.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004189)  
right-of-way payments,

**[03:23:19.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004190)  
yung interest payments po dito ay mabigat.

**[03:23:22.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004191)  
Every year po,

**[03:23:23.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004192)  
nag-incor tayo ng interest dito.

**[03:23:25.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004193)  
So 5 billion out of 90 billion,

**[03:23:28.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004194)  
malaking kakulangan po ang kailangan.

**[03:23:31.740] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004195)  
The reason why I'm asking,

**[03:23:33.420] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004196)  
kasi sa aking distrito,

**[03:23:35.800] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004197)  
marami po kasi kaming mga kalsada,

**[03:23:38.280] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004198)  
mga circumferential roads na sayang po,

**[03:23:39.860] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004199)  
hindi po magamit.

**[03:23:40.860] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004200)  
Gawa ng...

**[03:23:41.320] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004201)  
Yun din po ang right-of-way yung issue.

**[03:23:44.820] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004202)  
Nabigyan po ng,

**[03:23:45.820] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004203)  
kumbaga ng right-of-way,

**[03:23:47.380] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004204)  
pero dahil hindi na po nabayaran,

**[03:23:49.680] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004205)  
napigil na po yung construction.

**[03:23:52.800] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004206)  
So, kumbaga dati po,

**[03:23:54.400] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004207)  
binigyan ng access,

**[03:23:55.300] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004208)  
pero nahinto na po ngayon,

**[03:23:57.000] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004209)  
ayaw na po pumayag ng mga landowners.

**[03:24:00.340] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004211)  
Nandito po yung mga vice chairman natin ng appropriation.

**[03:24:03.880] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004212)  
Narinig naman po natin yung request ng ating sekretary.

**[03:24:08.120] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004213)  
Baka po mabigyan natin ng additional allocation

**[03:24:12.460] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004214)  
ang DPWH regarding right-of-way acquisition.

**[03:24:18.580] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004215)  
I'm speaking not just on my behalf.

**[03:24:22.240] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004216)  
I'm sure districts nationwide

**[03:24:24.660] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004217)  
are experiencing the same situation,

**[03:24:27.260] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004218)  
na maraming project ang nadedelay

**[03:24:31.060] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004219)  
dahil po sa right-of-way payments.

**[03:24:33.640] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004220)  
Yun lang po, Mr. Chair.

**[03:24:35.060] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004221)  
Thank you po.

**[03:24:35.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004222)  
That will be a very welcome development po,

**[03:24:37.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004223)  
Mr. Chair.

**[03:24:38.900] Rep. Ivan Howard A. Guintu** (asr-004224)  
Thank you.

**[03:24:39.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004225)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:24:40.360] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004226)  
Thank you. Thank you.

**[03:24:40.860] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004227)  
Kong Howard.

**[03:24:42.300] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004228)  
Next, from the minority,

**[03:24:44.520] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004229)  
Congressman Michael Tan.

**[03:24:48.580] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004230)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:24:50.340] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004231)  
And good afternoon po

**[03:24:52.140] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004232)  
to the secretary and the rest of the DPWH family.

**[03:24:56.420] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004233)  
So, una po, Sec,

**[03:24:58.040] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004234)  
gusto ko pong magpasalamat

**[03:24:59.640] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004235)  
kasi finally, after four years,

**[03:25:02.280] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004236)  
nabigyan po ng pondo.

**[03:25:04.600] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004237)  
As mentioned kanina ni Kong Stephen,

**[03:25:07.680] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004238)  
every year po,

**[03:25:09.740] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004239)  
yun at yun lang yung sinasabi namin dito.

**[03:25:13.600] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004240)  
And finally, nadinig na po yun.

**[03:25:17.160] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004241)  
Just to give you po an update,

**[03:25:19.200] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004242)  
yung tungkol naman po sa road right-of-way issues

**[03:25:22.840] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004243)  
along Maharlika Highway

**[03:25:25.080] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004244)  
ay inaasikaso na po ngayon ng provincial government.

**[03:25:28.440] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004245)  
Some of them are being paid through the provincial government.

**[03:25:31.700] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004246)  
But of course,

**[03:25:32.640] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004247)  
gusto din po naming humingi sa inyo

**[03:25:35.760] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004248)  
if you can allocate funds para doon.

**[03:25:38.700] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004249)  
But we are also doing kahit pa konti-konti

**[03:25:42.660] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004250)  
through the provincial government.

**[03:25:46.340] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004251)  
Secondly po,

**[03:25:47.280] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004252)  
itatanong ko nalang din po tungkol naman sa

**[03:25:51.160] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004253)  
maintenance ng Maharlika Highway

**[03:25:54.220] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004254)  
post-construction.

**[03:25:56.580] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004255)  
Kasi ano po ba yung mangyayari

**[03:25:58.460] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004256)  
if nandyan na po yung Maharlika Highway

**[03:26:01.460] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004257)  
at nagawa na po siya?

**[03:26:03.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004258)  
Unang-una po, maraming salamat, Kong Mike.

**[03:26:07.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004259)  
Alam ko po yung talagang passion ninyo,

**[03:26:09.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004260)  
talagang na maayos ng Maharlika Highway.

**[03:26:11.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004261)  
At salamat po sa tulong ninyo sa right-of-way.

**[03:26:14.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004262)  
Malaking bagay po yan.

**[03:26:15.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004263)  
Pero ngayon po talagang ano, ah

**[03:26:16.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004264)  
ang next phase na po natin sa Maharlika Highway,

**[03:26:20.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004265)  
pagkatapos po nating maayos,

**[03:26:21.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004266)  
kailangan na po tayong mag-widening.

**[03:26:24.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004268)  
At kailangan po natin yung right-of-way para dyan.

**[03:26:28.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004269)  
On the maintenance po,

**[03:26:29.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004270)  
maganda din pong tanong yan at napaka-importante.

**[03:26:32.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004271)  
Madalas din pong naka-cut ang routine maintenance budget

**[03:26:35.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004272)  
ng DPWH.

**[03:26:37.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004273)  
At dyan po kinukuha ang maintenance funds

**[03:26:40.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004274)  
para sa mga national roads natin.

**[03:26:43.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004275)  
Especially N1, especially Maharlika Highway.

**[03:26:46.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004276)  
Kaya po, hihingi din po kami ng tulong na sana po,

**[03:26:49.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004277)  
e wag din pong sanang mabawasan ang ating maintenance funds.

**[03:26:53.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004278)  
Dahil critical din po yan

**[03:26:54.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004279)  
sa pag-maintain natin ang ating national highways.

**[03:26:57.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004280)  
Pero tuloy-tuloy po tayo,

**[03:27:00.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004281)  
Mr. Chairman, Congressman,

**[03:27:03.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004282)  
sisiguraduhin natin ang ma-maintain natin na maayos

**[03:27:06.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004283)  
at masalimuot tayo sa pag-maintain.

**[03:27:08.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004284)  
In fact, ngayon nga po,

**[03:27:10.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004285)  
talagang ano po,

**[03:27:10.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004286)  
yung dati pong tinatawag nilang patsi-patsi lang,

**[03:27:14.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004287)  
e talagang ginagamit na po natin yung mga methodologies

**[03:27:18.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004288)  
na nakikita natin for routine maintenance

**[03:27:20.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004289)  
sa ibang bansa.

**[03:27:22.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004290)  
Para masigurado po na kahit papano naman,

**[03:27:24.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004291)  
itatagal po ang mga maintenance works na ginagawa natin

**[03:27:29.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004292)  
lalo na sa mga national highways natin.

**[03:27:32.080] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004293)  
Thank you.

**[03:27:33.100] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004294)  
And sana po,

**[03:27:34.280] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004295)  
yung post-construction matutukan talaga yung maintenance

**[03:27:37.860] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004296)  
because during the time po na umabot sa part na nasira na talaga,

**[03:27:44.020] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004297)  
na patsi-patsi na talaga yung Maharlika Highway,

**[03:27:47.100] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004298)  
it started in small segments lang.

**[03:27:49.480] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004299)  
That if nagawan lang sana,

**[03:27:51.460] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004300)  
ng paraan,

**[03:27:52.340] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004301)  
through maintenance

**[03:27:53.400] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004302)  
or baka yung section na yun inayos,

**[03:27:56.360] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004303)  
hindi siya parang naging parang nagsangasanga

**[03:27:59.740] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004304)  
hanggang lumaki nang lumaki nang lumaki.

**[03:28:02.040] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004305)  
So, I hope that the good secretary and the DPWH

**[03:28:06.120] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004306)  
can do something about it post-construction po.

**[03:28:10.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004307)  
Well, we will do that, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:28:12.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004308)  
Sana po hindi na maulit yung nasisira ulit

**[03:28:16.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004309)  
at ma-maintain po natin ang mabuti

**[03:28:19.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004310)  
ang napaka-importante nating Maharlika Highway

**[03:28:21.620] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004312)  
Okay. Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:28:23.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004313)  
With your support po, sir. Thank you po.

**[03:28:25.900] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004314)  
My next topic po is,

**[03:28:27.800] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004315)  
dati po nagre-reklamo ako tungkol sa Maharlika Highway

**[03:28:30.940] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004316)  
kasi po hindi siya napupondohan

**[03:28:33.400] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004317)  
or hindi sapat yung pondo na inabibigay sa Maharlika Highway.

**[03:28:38.320] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004318)  
Pero sec, masyado ata ang ginalingan niyo yung sa Maharlika Highway

**[03:28:42.140] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004319)  
na yung nangyari naman ngayon,

**[03:28:44.140] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004320)  
wala na pong local projects na pondo para sa distrito ko po.

**[03:28:47.840] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004321)  
So ngayon po, the problem naman ng distrito ko is

**[03:28:52.300] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004322)  
lahat po ng pondo ay nasa Maharlika Highway na

**[03:28:56.140] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004323)  
wala na po tayong local roads, wala na po tayong hospital projects,

**[03:29:02.940] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004324)  
wala na po tayong MPV, wala na po tayong drainage canal,

**[03:29:08.940] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004325)  
wala na po mga flood mitigation programs para dun po sa distrito ko po.

**[03:29:13.860] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004326)  
And in the past, in the previous years,

**[03:29:17.920] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004327)  
alam naman po natin na hindi lang naman po,

**[03:29:20.300] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004328)  
highway lang din po yung problema ng Samar.

**[03:29:23.260] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004329)  
Malaki po yung area ng Samar.

**[03:29:25.120] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004330)  
We have 524 barangays in my district alone.

**[03:29:29.370] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004331)  
We have 15 municipalities and 1 city with 500,000 na population.

**[03:29:37.260] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004332)  
May dalawa po kaming municipality na islands.

**[03:29:42.240] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004333)  
Meron din kaming mga GIDA areas.

**[03:29:44.480] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004334)  
And since wala pong pondo ngayon for the local projects,

**[03:29:48.600] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004343)  
yun naman po yung magiging problema namin.

**[03:29:51.420] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004344)  
So, I would ask or seek the clarification from the Secretary

**[03:29:56.200] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004345)  
kung bakit po nagkaganon and how can we rectify this problem po.

**[03:30:05.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004346)  
Tama po yun.

**[03:30:07.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004347)  
Limitado din po talaga ang fiscal space natin.

**[03:30:10.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004348)  
At ang primary mandate po ng DPWH talaga

**[03:30:15.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004349)  
ay talagang ayusin natin ang ating mga national roads.

**[03:30:19.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004350)  
Pero naiintindihan ko po as we discussed yesterday

**[03:30:21.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004351)  
kung kailangan din naman ang local roads.

**[03:30:26.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004352)  
And kinausap ko na po ang ating regional director

**[03:30:29.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004353)  
at ang mga district engineers na mag-ano din po.

**[03:30:34.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004354)  
Kung pwede pong mag-ano tayo, magkaroon tayo ng mga

**[03:30:38.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004355)  
especially yung mga continuing,

**[03:30:40.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004356)  
yung mga ongoing po na projects natin sa inyong distrito.

**[03:30:43.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004357)  
At yung napaka-importante po nating tri-boundary roads

**[03:30:47.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004358)  
na pinag-usapan po natin nung ako po'y nandun sa Region 8,

**[03:30:52.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004359)  
eh mapondohan din po.

**[03:30:54.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004360)  
But ang primary mandate po talaga ng DPWH yung national roads

**[03:30:59.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004361)  
pero ano naman po, pwede naman po tayo.

**[03:31:02.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004362)  
Kailangan nga po talaga masigurado natin

**[03:31:05.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004363)  
na magpa-prioritize tayo ng ating continuing.

**[03:31:07.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004364)  
At the same time, syempre po,

**[03:31:09.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004365)  
kumpleto po lahat ng ating mga requirements sa lahat ng mga proyekto.

**[03:31:13.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004366)  
So, okay po yun.

**[03:31:16.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004367)  
Importante din naman po yung mga local roads,

**[03:31:18.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004368)  
especially for GIDA areas na kailangan-kailangan ng infrastructure.

**[03:31:22.960] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004369)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[03:31:26.220] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004370)  
Umaasa po ako na sana ma-address po itong concern ko

**[03:31:30.100] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004371)  
kasi it will be a big problem also for us,

**[03:31:33.800] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004372)  
especially in Samar, na as mentioned kanina,

**[03:31:36.660] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004373)  
malaki po yung area namin.

**[03:31:38.360] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004374)  
And because of that, kung wala po tayong mga local roads,

**[03:31:43.740] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004375)  
itong mga far-flung baranggays would have a very difficult time,

**[03:31:48.640] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004376)  
especially itong mga island municipalities also.

**[03:31:52.520] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004377)  
Also po, gusto ko lang din po, again, to thank the DPWH family

**[03:32:04.340] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004378)  
and hopefully ito pong mga concern namin sa

**[03:32:09.820] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004379)  
Local.

**[03:32:12.360] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004380)  
Pinakikinggan nyo po ito kasi during the time that we were seeking your assistance

**[03:32:17.060] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004381)  
for the Maharlika Highway, nakailang punta po kayo sa Samar.

**[03:32:21.520] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004382)  
Siguro nga po parang nagsasawa na rin kayo sa amin

**[03:32:24.780] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004383)  
kasi lagi nyo na po kaming nakikita.

**[03:32:29.460] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004384)  
Pero hindi lang po talaga Maharlika Highway yung problem namin.

**[03:32:33.700] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004385)  
So, I hope that yung na-raise ko pong concern about the local projects

**[03:32:39.860] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004386)  
is ma-address po natin.

**[03:32:42.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004387)  
So, makakaasa po kayo, Mr. Chairman, Kong Mike.

**[03:32:45.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004388)  
And makakaasa po din po ako na hindi ako magsasawa at babalikan po natin ang Samar

**[03:32:49.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004389)  
dahil kailangan po talaga ng mga kababayan natin doon yung infrastructure na maayos.

**[03:32:56.480] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004390)  
Thank you po.

**[03:32:57.500] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004391)  
And lastly, thank you din po sa Calviga Bridge.

**[03:33:00.320] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004392)  
At least ngayon, nadadaanan na po.

**[03:33:02.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004393)  
Opo.

**[03:33:03.100] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004394)  
Malaking tulong po yun.

**[03:33:04.320] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004395)  
And also the San Juanico Bridge po.

**[03:33:07.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004396)  
Ay, Liloan pala nasa baba.

**[03:33:08.080] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004397)  
O, San Juanico Bridge po.

**[03:33:11.740] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004399)  
Nadadaanan na po rin.

**[03:33:12.680] Rep. Reynolds Michael T. Tan** (asr-004400)  
Thank you po.

**[03:33:14.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004401)  
Maraming salamat.

**[03:33:15.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004403)  
At ayusin pa rin po natin tuloy-tuloy ang Calbiga Bridge para po.

**[03:33:18.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004404)  
Awa ng Diyos, hindi naman po tayo magka-aberya

**[03:33:23.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004405)  
Salamat po.

**[03:33:23.900] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004406)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[03:33:24.440] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-004407)  
Next, from the majority, Congressman Salvador Pleyto.

**[03:33:31.340] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004408)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:33:34.560] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004409)  
Magandang hapon po sa DPWH family.

**[03:33:38.000] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004410)  
Family, headed by walang kapaguran na Secretary Vince, na hindi yata natutulog na po ito.

**[03:33:46.460] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004423)  
Nakikita naman namin, Secretary, mula sa mga bundok ng Cotelera,

**[03:33:53.120] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004424)  
at Alcima Highway, papunta ka dito sa Marcos Highway, dito sa Cailin Road,

**[03:33:57.740] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004425)  
papunta sa mga bumagsak na Maharlika, dito sa mga bumagsak na tuloy ninyo sa Visayas,

**[03:34:04.420] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004426)  
hanggang sa mga road trips na Mindanao, kung saan saan pa.

**[03:34:08.220] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004427)  
Ang una kong tanong, Secretary, nakakatulog po ba kayo?

**[03:34:12.640] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004428)  
para tumahan.

**[03:34:13.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004429)  
Oo naman po.

**[03:34:15.660] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004430)  
Kasi nung pumasok po kayo dito,

**[03:34:17.180] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004431)  
slim na kayo,

**[03:34:17.840] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004432)  
mas slim pa kayo ngayon.

**[03:34:20.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004433)  
Trabaho lang po.

**[03:34:21.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004434)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[03:34:22.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004435)  
Tuloy-tuloy.

**[03:34:24.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004436)  
Habang buhay po.

**[03:34:25.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004437)  
Habang buhay po.

**[03:34:26.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004438)  
Kanina umaga pa po ako dito.

**[03:34:27.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004439)  
Mr. Chairman

**[03:34:28.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004440)  
Pag patay na eh.

**[03:34:29.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004441)  
Wala na.

**[03:34:29.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004442)  
Balik-balik lang po kami

**[03:34:30.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004443)  
kasi sabay-sabay

**[03:34:31.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004444)  
tatlo briefing

**[03:34:32.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004445)  
saka kay speaker.

**[03:34:34.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004446)  
Diyan lang kami galing ngayon.

**[03:34:35.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004447)  
May mga concern sila

**[03:34:37.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004448)  
kaya pinatawag po ako doon

**[03:34:38.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004449)  
mamaya.

**[03:34:39.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004450)  
Siguro babanggitin ko na rin mamaya

**[03:34:41.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004451)  
kasi may clearance naman po ako sa kanya.

**[03:34:42.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-004452)  
Saka kay Majo.

**[03:34:45.100] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004453)  
Una kong pansekretary,

**[03:34:47.400] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-004454)  
we welcome the initiative

**[03:55:48.500] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005071)

**[03:55:50.080] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005072)

**[03:55:52.480] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005073)

**[03:55:59.520] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005076)

**[03:56:01.020] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005077)

**[03:56:03.040] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005078)

**[03:56:05.140] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005079)

**[03:56:09.200] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005081)

**[03:56:10.160] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005082)

**[03:56:13.160] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005083)

**[03:56:15.100] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005084)

**[03:56:17.580] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005085)

**[03:56:19.360] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005086)

**[03:56:20.800] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005087)  
and a comprehensive and coordinated

**[03:56:22.780] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005088)  
land management program

**[03:56:25.040] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005089)  
Lastly, hindi natin dapat

**[03:56:27.340] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005090)

**[03:56:29.700] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005091)  
Ang tunay na Kontra-Baha

**[03:56:31.340] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005092)  
ay prevention, preparedness,

**[03:56:33.360] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005093)  
at permanenting solusyon.

**[03:56:34.680] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005094)  
To President Ferdinand Marcos Jr.,

**[03:56:37.480] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005095)  
maraming salamat po for making

**[03:56:38.960] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005096)

To Secretary Vince Dizon and

**[03:56:46.960] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005100)  
men and women of DPWH,

**[03:56:48.800] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005101)  
MMDA, San Miguel Corporation,

Mayor April Aguilar,

maraming salamat po

**[03:56:55.220] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005104)

**[03:56:58.020] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005105)  
sa mga taga-Las Piñas.

**[03:56:59.280] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005106)  
This is a proof when national

**[03:57:01.760] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005107)

**[03:57:05.320] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005109)  
mas mabilis ang aksyon at

**[03:57:07.540] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005110)  
mas maraming mamamayan ang

**[03:57:09.440] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005111)  
matutulungan. Sa laban kontra baha,

**[03:57:11.720] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005112)

**[03:57:13.820] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005113)

**[03:57:17.040] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005115)

**[03:57:19.740] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005116)  
ang sama-samang pagkilos,

**[03:57:21.120] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005117)

**[03:57:23.640] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005118)  
at higit sa lahat,

**[03:57:25.080] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005119)  
pang matagalang solusyon.

**[03:57:27.240] Rep. Mark Anthony G. Santos** (asr-005120)

**[03:57:33.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005123)

**[03:58:01.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005136)

**[03:58:09.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005140)

**[03:58:11.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005141)  
manifestation of my support

**[03:58:13.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005142)  
to the DPWH family.

**[03:58:15.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005143)

**[03:58:17.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005144)

**[03:58:19.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005145)  
Because we want

**[03:58:21.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005146)  
to be

**[03:58:21.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005147)  
clarified in my district

**[03:58:25.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005148)  
or even the province of

**[03:58:27.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005149)  
Camarines Sur.

**[03:58:30.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005150)  
What is the

**[03:58:31.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005151)  
difference of excavation

**[03:58:33.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005152)

**[03:58:38.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005153)

**[03:58:40.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005154)  
So, ito na lang po,

**[03:58:41.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005155)  
just to make it simple.

**[03:58:43.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005156)  
Line canal,

**[03:58:47.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005157)  
excavation.

**[03:58:47.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005158)

**[03:58:48.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005159)  
So, hindi po pwedeng

**[03:58:50.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005160)  
i-confiscate

**[03:58:51.600] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005161)  
ng any agency

**[03:58:54.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005162)  
kung may ginagawa

**[03:58:56.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005163)  
ng paggawa ng line canal.

**[03:58:59.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005164)  
Basta po may permit to excavate,

**[03:59:01.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005165)  
hindi po puwede.

**[03:59:02.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005166)  
Yes. I'm talking about national government

**[03:59:05.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005167)  
projects.

**[03:59:06.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005168)  
Ay po, kung national government project po,

**[03:59:08.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005169)  
Yes

**[03:59:09.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005170)  
Just to inform you, Mr. Chair,

**[03:59:11.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005171)  
sa amin po kasi,

**[03:59:14.820] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005172)

**[03:59:17.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005173)  
Okay ah

**[03:59:18.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005174)  
I think you are familiar.

**[03:59:19.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005175)  
We have a letter already.

**[03:59:22.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005176)  
This is not,

**[03:59:23.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005177)  
it is beyond politics

**[03:59:24.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005178)  
because any development,

**[03:59:27.300] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005179)  
any project is for the good of

**[03:59:29.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005180)  
the people also.

**[03:59:30.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005181)  
So, I'm just asking help from the department.

**[03:59:33.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005182)  
It is going to be an interagency,

**[03:59:35.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005183)  
I think.

**[03:59:37.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005184)  
DENR with PNP.

**[03:59:39.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005185)  
Because ang nangyari po sa amin,

**[03:59:41.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005186)  
last week lang,

**[03:59:42.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005187)  
Kaya meron pong,

**[03:59:43.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005188)  
actually, hindi na po sa inyo,

**[03:59:46.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005189)  
meron pong

**[03:59:47.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005198)  
fishpond lang sa private, kaya gawa po ng fishpond,

**[03:59:52.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005199)  
kinukuha po ng Sagip Kalikasan Task Force, tao po ng Kapitolyo.

**[03:59:57.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005200)  
And we don't know the reason why.

**[04:00:00.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005201)  
And may ginagawa pong daan, recently, last week, na karamoan,

**[04:00:06.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005202)  
Siyempre po, pag magawa kayo ng daan, gagamit ka po ng bako, hinuhuli rin po yun.

**[04:00:13.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005203)  
So, ano po yung measures na pwedeng maitulong ng DPWH sa mga winning bidders,

**[04:00:22.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005204)  
sa mga contractors natin?

**[04:00:24.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005205)  
Unang-una po nung natanggap ko nga po yung sulat, Mr. Chairman, Kong Arnie,

**[04:00:29.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005206)  
at dumulog po ako sa DENR, kay Sekretary Cuna, para po mapag-usapan na kung ano ba ang rules.

**[04:00:36.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005207)  
Kasi yung enforcement po nito, tagay kang nasa party na po ng DENR.

**[04:00:42.800] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005208)  
Pero, ang ayaw na ayaw po natin, magka-abirya at maapektuhan ang mga national projects.

**[04:00:51.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005209)  
Hindi lang po ng DPWH, kundi pati po ng mga ibang ahensya.

**[04:00:55.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005210)  
Tugay na, mabawa, sa DOTR, yung po sa airport sa Naga, mayroon din po mga reports.

**[04:01:02.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005211)  
Although, mukhang okay na po yun.

**[04:01:05.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005212)  
Sana po.

**[04:01:06.160] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005213)  
Yung po, mukhang tuloy-tuloy na po at wala na pong aberya ngayon.

**[04:01:09.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005214)  
Pero po,

**[04:01:10.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005215)  
kailangan po talagang mag-usap-usap ang mga ahensya, lalo na ang DENR, para po klaro ang policy on these issues.

**[04:01:18.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005216)  
So, makakaasa po kayo na tuloy-tuloy po akong makikipagdulog sa DENR para po makahanap na po tayo.

**[04:01:25.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005217)  
Yes, salamat po. Pero, any local ordinance, I became a mayor also,

**[04:01:33.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005218)  
pag magawa po tayo ng batas locally, we cannot supersede any national law.

**[04:01:38.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005219)  
Tama po.

**[04:01:39.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005220)  
So, even confiscation.

**[04:01:40.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005221)  
impounding, nangyayari po sa amin yan.

**[04:01:43.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005222)  
Ang kumukuha po is Sagip Kalikasan Task Force.

**[04:01:46.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005223)  
And para makuha nyo is mag-file po kayo ng kaso po sa korte.

**[04:01:50.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005224)  
And alam naman po natin yung justice system natin, pag-file pa lang, pagbayad, etc.

**[04:01:55.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005225)  
But, with regards to this matter, medyo malala na po yung nangyayari sa amin.

**[04:02:02.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005226)  
In fact, may isa pong, I won't mention the name, but may isa pong contractor, anim na bako na po, ang kinuha.

**[04:02:10.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005227)  
Dahil po gumagawa sa aming boulevard, and that is the Partido Boulevard along the coastline.

**[04:02:17.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005228)  
So, siyempre po may bako ka para gumawa ng pundasyon, kailangan pong gamitin yun, kinukonfiscate po yun.

**[04:02:27.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005229)  
So, I don't know, this is beyond politics, sir.

**[04:02:32.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005230)  
So, I don't want you to be involved in local politics, but this is a national project already.

**[04:02:39.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005231)  
There are abuses of Sangib Kalikasan Task Force, na ang alam ko po talaga, even DNR, not only DNR, but the PNP, it's a composite team.

**[04:02:50.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005232)  
Dapat po deputize ka, kasama po yung DNR, pag-implement, and even po ang ating kapolisan.

**[04:02:56.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005233)  
But, thank you, at least you are very well aware of the situation.

**[04:03:02.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005234)  
Lalo na po, minsan mga line canal, re-blocking.

**[04:03:08.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005235)  
Minsan po, pag sa amin nag-landslide, may landslide, pag nag-clearing ka ng daan, tinatawag na po yun, quarrying.

**[04:03:18.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005236)  
So, that is the situation.

**[04:03:20.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005237)  
I don't know kung sa amin lang pong distrito yan, or sa buong pong Pilipinas.

**[04:03:25.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005238)  
So, I hope we can stop the abuse na ginagawa po, na nangyayari po sa amin.

**[04:03:32.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005239)  
So, dumudulog lang po kami sa inyo, sir.

**[04:03:34.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005240)  
Opo, makakaasa po kayo, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:03:37.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005241)  
Kasi, ayaw na ayaw po natin.

**[04:03:39.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005242)  
yung nade-delay ang mga proyekto kasi kailangan-kailangan po ito ng mga kababayan natin.

**[04:03:43.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005243)  
Thank you, Chair. That's it. I'm fully supportive of your leadership.

**[04:03:48.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-005244)  
Thank you very much.

**[04:03:49.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005245)  
Thank you Cong Arnie, thank you,

**[04:03:50.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-005246)  
Mr. Chairman.

Thank you, thank you, Kong Arnie.

if I'm not mistaken, ano,

**[04:05:23.940] Rep. Esmael G. Mangudadatu** (asr-005261)

ang naialat doon sa stretch na yun

**[04:05:33.480] Rep. Esmael G. Mangudadatu** (asr-005263)

**[05:18:55.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006961)  
yung pong dating pinag-iit,

**[05:18:57.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006962)  
eh ngayon, nung nasira,

**[05:18:59.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006963)  
pinagkakinaho namin ulit po.

**[05:19:01.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006964)  
Agree po ako diyan, 100%.

**[05:19:02.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006965)  
Thank you very much po.

**[05:19:04.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006966)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[05:19:05.540] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006967)  
Thank you.

**[05:19:06.260] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006968)  
Next, from the majority,

**[05:19:07.960] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-006969)  
a short manifestation from Congresswoman Ana Fondo.

**[05:19:11.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-006970)  
Thank you so much po.

**[05:19:14.060] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-006971)  
Sec, can you tell us more about dredging?

**[05:19:18.880] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-006972)  
And what's the policy na po?

**[05:19:20.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-006973)  
I would like to thank Director Ilao and Usec. Calima po

**[05:19:25.700] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-006974)  
for the dredging na tinulong po nila sa amin.

**[05:19:29.300] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-006975)  
So, if you could just share a little po.

**[05:19:31.920] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006976)  
Apo.

**[05:19:32.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006977)  
Very critical po.

**[05:19:34.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006978)  
Ang dredging at desilting.

**[05:19:37.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006979)  
Simply because medyo mayigit isang dekada na po

**[05:19:41.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006980)  
since talagang nag-full blast ang dredging and desilting program

**[05:19:46.200] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006981)  
ng DPWH for various reasons,

**[05:19:48.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006982)  
which hindi na po ako mag-de-dwell doon.

**[05:19:51.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006983)  
Pero nung kami po ay unang pumasok,

**[05:19:54.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006984)  
kinonsulta po namin ang mga eksperto

**[05:19:57.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006985)  
from Project NOAH to UP,

**[05:20:02.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006986)  
pati po si Mr. Ramon Ang ng San Miguel Corporation.

**[05:20:06.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006987)  
Sinabi po nilang lahat na ang pinakamabilis,

**[05:20:10.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006988)  
pinaka-mura,

**[05:20:13.300] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006989)  
at pinaka-effective na initial intervention sa flood mitigation

**[05:20:18.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006990)  
ay ang pag-dredge, desilt, at pag-ilinis ng mga waterways.

**[05:20:22.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006991)  
At hindi lang po dredging ng major rivers,

**[05:20:24.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006992)  
kundi pati po mga magigit na waterways tulad ng mga estero,

**[05:20:28.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006993)  
ay kailangan i-desilt.

**[05:20:29.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006994)  
Kaya po nag-launch po tayo nung November,

**[05:20:32.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006995)  
ng operant contra baja kasama po si Pangulo.

**[05:20:34.760] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006996)  
At nationwide po ito.

**[05:20:36.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006997)  
At nakita na po natin ang mga epekto,

**[05:20:39.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006998)  
hindi lang sa NCR, kundi sa Northern Luzon, sa Cagayan,

**[05:20:43.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-006999)  
sa Tuguegarao, nakita po natin agad ang epekto doon.

**[05:20:46.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007000)  
Nakita po natin kahit papano ang epekto sa Central Luzon.

**[05:20:50.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007001)  
Pero kailangan po kasi sa dredging, hindi po ito one-time lang.

**[05:20:56.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007002)  
Kailangan po ito, at ito po ang paulit-ulit na sinasabi ng ating Pangulo,

**[05:21:00.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007003)  
this needs to be a regular maintenance program of the DPWH.

**[05:21:06.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007004)  
Kaya po, yun na po ang gusto nating mangyari ngayon.

**[05:21:09.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007005)  
Simula po ngayong taon, tuloy-tuloy na po,

**[05:21:12.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007006)  
kaya kami po bumibili ng equipment,

**[05:21:15.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007007)  
lalo na po yung mga magigit na backhoe on barge

**[05:21:19.080] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007008)  
na pwedeng pumasok sa kahit na mga magigit na estero,

**[05:21:22.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007009)  
eh kailangan na po nating bilhin yan.

**[05:21:25.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007010)  
Dahil napaka-importante pong investment yan.

**[05:21:27.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007011)  
At hindi na po natin pwedeng ipagpag-ibad ito.

**[05:21:32.440] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007012)  
So yun po, tuloy-tuloy na po ito, ma'am.

**[05:21:34.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007013)  
At makakaasa po kayo, we are constantly building up our capacity

**[05:21:38.980] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007014)  
to keep on dredging and dredging and cleaning our waterways.

**[05:21:42.140] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007015)  
Thank you so much po.

**[05:21:44.260] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007016)  
And last na last na talaga.

**[05:21:46.900] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007017)  
I would just like to remind to, ano na po,

**[05:21:49.220] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007018)  
not to remind but to just make aware.  Opo.

**[05:21:54.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007019)  
Marami po tayong foreign assisted projects.

**[05:21:57.020] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007020)  
Regarding flood control.

**[05:21:59.220] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007021)  
But the easiest po and the one that can happen po

**[05:22:02.680] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007022)  
in less than six months,

**[05:22:04.400] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007023)  
Director Fano, kayo po yun.

**[05:22:06.620] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007024)  
Yung ating po Korean loan part two.

**[05:22:09.280] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007025)  
Ngayon ho, the President already said po

**[05:22:12.800] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007026)  
na gagawin po yung Korean, kasi siya po ang nag-inaugurate ng part one.

**[05:22:17.900] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007027)  
So, sayang naman po, again, an unfinished project.

**[05:22:21.660] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007028)  
May part two po ito.

**[05:22:22.980] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007029)  
And nakikipagtulungan po tayo with Director Fano.

**[05:22:25.820] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007030)  
Paalala ko lang,

**[05:22:26.640] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007031)  
in less than six months po,

**[05:22:28.600] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007032)  
tutukan lang po natin we can have a very productive loan po

**[05:22:32.860] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007033)  
from Korea.

**[05:22:33.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007034)  
So, paalala lang po.

**[05:22:36.520] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007035)  
And I'm sure si Director Fano will share all the details po with us.

**[05:22:40.860] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007036)  
Nakakuha na po tayo ng RDC approval.

**[05:22:42.660] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007037)  
And susunod na po ay dadalhin na po natin sa DepDev.

**[05:22:46.000] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007038)  
At papaspasan po natin ito para huma-approve.

**[05:22:49.020] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007039)  
Thank you so much po.

**[05:22:50.320] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007040)  
And again, thank you Director Fano.

**[05:22:52.940] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007041)  
And Yusek Sadaen.

**[05:22:54.820] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007042)  
So sa inyo po lahat, again, maraming maraming salamat po.

**[05:22:57.760] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007044)  
And we appreciate all your efforts po.

**[05:23:00.820] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007045)  
Kahit kami po ay nahihirapan.

**[05:23:02.920] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007046)  
Ang feeling namin, nakatulong po kayo na malaki sa amin.

**[05:23:05.860] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007047)  
And we wish to continue to work with you.

**[05:23:09.520] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007048)  
Full support po ang 4th District of Pampanga sa inyo pong budget.

**[05:23:13.740] Rep. Anna York P. Bondoc, M.D.** (asr-007049)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:23:16.000] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007050)  
Thank you, Kong Anna.

**[05:23:18.760] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007051)  
Before I call on the last interpellator from the minority.

**[05:23:23.260] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007052)  
Secretary, marami sana akong tanong sa inyo.

**[05:23:25.700] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007053)  
Ang problema, akong magdidipinsan ng budget inyo.

**[05:23:28.260] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007054)  
So hindi na lang akong magtatalong, di ba?

**[05:23:29.740] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007055)  
So, ang, manghihingi na lang kami ng tulong.

**[05:23:34.000] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007056)  
Yung distrito ninyo sa Surigao del Sur,

**[05:23:36.060] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007057)  
yung 1st Surigao del Sur Engineering District,

**[05:23:39.160] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007058)  
after Basiang po, hanggang ngayon, maraming section pa rin,

**[05:23:44.680] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007059)  
mountainous section along Surigao de Abao Coastal Road,

**[05:23:47.340] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007060)  
ang kalahati lang po ang mataanan.

**[05:23:49.620] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007061)  
Yung mga 10-wheeler trucks, transporting cargos, cannot pass through the National Highway.

**[05:23:55.960] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007062)  
The problem is, Secretary, marami akong kalaban sa politika.

**[05:24:01.800] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007063)  
At sinabi nila doon, akala namin manakas na dahil ikaw yung chairman ng Committee on Public Works.

**[05:24:06.660] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007064)  
Hanggang ngayon ho, isang taon na halos hindi madaan ng kumpito yung aming kalsada dahil half-lane lang po.

**[05:24:14.100] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007065)  
So, if you can, may binigay na sa iyo si Clara na 40 million.

**[05:24:17.860] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007066)  
Doon sa immediate response fund.

**[05:24:21.840] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007067)  
Pero ang hiningi yata ng inyong District Engineering Office po, is 300 million.

**[05:24:27.880] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007068)  
Baka ho, para hindi na tayo mapuna doon sa lugar na iyon.

**[05:24:32.700] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007069)  
Thank you so much, Mr. Secretary.

**[05:24:39.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007070)  
Ano kakalampagin po namin ang release nung in-apply namin sa QRF?

**[05:24:45.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007071)  
Tututukan ko po ito, Mr. Chairman, para po mamaspasa natin.

**[05:24:48.740] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007072)  
Sana po.

**[05:24:49.320] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007073)  
Early possible time, dahil medyo tag-ulan na naman. Baka tamaan na naman binang tag-ulan.

**[05:24:58.440] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007074)  
This is main trunk line connecting Surigao del Norte, Surigao del Sur, Dabao Oriental, Dabao Norte, at saka Dabao City.

**[05:25:08.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007075)  
You have our commitment, Mr. Chairman.

**[05:25:10.020] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007076)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary. Thank you so much.

**[05:25:12.120] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007078)  
Now, I will call on the last interpellator, Congressman Antonio Tinio from the minority.

**[05:25:18.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007079)  
Thank you, Chair.

**[05:25:20.740] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007080)  
Last interpellator for round 2.

**[05:25:22.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007081)  
Tama po.

**[05:25:24.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007082)  
Anyway, yung ano po, yan, okay.

**[05:25:31.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007083)  
Pagpapatuloy lang ito.

**[05:25:33.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007084)  
Kanina, tinalakay natin yung budget na malaki sa central office, mga proyekto hanggang sa region.

**[05:25:42.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007085)  
Ngayon naman, may observation kami sa mga proyekto na nasa mga district engineering offices.

**[05:25:49.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007086)  
Sa bilang namin, 199 ng DEOs. Tama ba?

**[05:25:54.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007087)  
Is that correct?

**[05:25:57.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007088)  
Correct. 199 DEOs.

**[05:26:01.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007089)  
Nakita namin sa ilang mga programa, hindi lahat ng DEO merong project.

**[05:26:07.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007090)  
So, for example, under flood management program, 87 DEOs. Walang flood control.

**[05:26:15.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007091)  
Tapos, for example, sa BIP multipurpose buildings,

**[05:26:20.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007092)  
37 DEOs. Walang proposal for multipurpose buildings.

**[05:26:27.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007093)  
So, parang bungi-bungi yung sa mga DEOs. Ano po ang explanation doon?

**[05:26:34.210] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007094)  
Ang paliwanag po, Mr. Chairman, is meron pong mga, una yung mga DEOs po na nag-submit.

**[05:26:41.720] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007095)  
Sila po yung nag-submit ng mga kumpletong rekisitos based doon sa bago nating mga reforma.

**[05:26:47.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007096)  
At yun naman pong mga, pero hindi naman po ibig sabihin na wala silang i-implement na flood mitigation program.

**[05:26:55.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007097)  
Meron po dito, nasa region po, pero ito din po ay depende sa type of project, ibababa din po sa mga district offices.

**[05:27:07.340] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007098)  
Kaya po, halimbawa po yung sa region 11, most of the projects po are in the central and regional office.

**[05:27:15.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007099)  
Totaling roughly about 2.1 billion. Pero yan naman po, pagdating ng implementation ay ibababa din po yan sa mga DEOs para sila ang mag-i-implement.

**[05:27:28.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007100)  
Okay. So, ito actually yung tanong ko. May narinig tayo mga congressman, sinasabi nila, nako yung proyekto ko hindi lumabas. Wala dyan.

**[05:27:42.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007101)  
May proyekto pero hindi galing sa akin. Sa central office galing. Okay.

**[05:27:47.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007102)  
Mr. Chairman, I just want to correct the misconception na ang project ay central o ano. Lahat po ng mga proyekto natin ay nanggagaling sa baba.

**[05:28:00.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007103)  
Ang central po ay hindi nagdidikta ng proyekto. Hindi ko po alam dati ha. I cannot speak for the past. Pero dito po sa ginawa nating proseso ngayon, lahat po ng proyekto ay nanggagaling sa district, region,

**[05:28:17.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007104)  
focusing on continuing and finishing the unfinished, closing the gaps if they are flood control, and continuing other projects such as pumping stations, and the like.

**[05:28:30.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007105)  
The only reason po na lumagabas sa NEP na sa central po is dahil po sa mga existing circulars na kinakailangan po ang baba ng mga budget authorities,

**[05:28:45.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007106)  
authorities, yung SARO po na tinatawag natin, e ibababa muna sa Central, DPWH, at yun po ay ibababa sa mga implementing offices.

**[05:28:56.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007107)  
Pero yung pong mga proyektong yun ay nanggagaling lahat sa baba.

**[05:29:01.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007108)  
Okay, siguro ang ibig sabihin nila doon, hindi sila ang proponent.

**[05:29:04.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007109)  
Okay, now, ang tanong ko actually is this.

**[05:29:11.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007110)  
In the past, in the 2026 budget, nagsubmit ang DPWH ng extensive ERATA.

**[05:29:20.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007111)  
Alam nyo yun, di ba?

**[05:29:20.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007112)  
So, maraming mga nagbago talaga yung itsura ng budget na submit nyo.

**[05:29:26.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007113)  
Tanong ko, magsasubmit ba uli ng ERATA ang DPWH ngayon?

**[05:29:36.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007114)  
Ang gagawin po namin ngayon ay base na rin po sa discussions ngayon, mga suggestions na gumabas.

**[05:29:42.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007115)  
pati na rin po yung mga priorities na hindi po naipasok dito sa NEP

**[05:29:50.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007116)  
dahil po sa mga priorities na nakita natin itong mga nakaraang abagat,

**[05:29:54.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007117)  
magsasubmit po kami for consideration po ng House Appropriation Committees as Institutional Amendments.

**[05:30:01.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007118)  
Pero yun po ay i-justify namin at i-deditagye po namin with the same documentary requirements

**[05:30:08.880] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007119)  
that we required dito po sa mga sinabmit ngayong National Expenditure Program.

**[05:30:15.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007120)  
Okay, so yes, magkakaroon ng irata. In other words, yung NEP na sinabmit nyo, magbabago pa yan,

**[05:30:21.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007121)  
and will there be extensive changes?

**[05:30:24.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007122)  
I don't think it will be extensive changes.

**[05:30:26.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007123)  
And we will make sure po na lahat po ng isisumiti namin ay justified based on need

**[05:30:34.240] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007124)  
and based on the required documentation.

**[05:30:36.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007125)  
Okay.

**[05:30:41.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007126)

**[05:30:58.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007134)

**[05:31:07.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007137)

**[05:31:15.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007139)

**[05:31:15.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007140)

**[05:31:18.980] Rep. Romeo S. Momo Sr.** (asr-007141)

**[05:31:20.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007142)

**[05:31:26.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007143)

**[05:31:28.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007144)

**[05:31:30.560] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007145)

**[05:31:34.120] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007146)  
Yun po ang initial na plano namin.

**[05:31:40.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007147)  
So, okay.

**[05:31:41.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007148)  
Explain the, sabi ng legal nyo, explain the

**[05:31:44.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007149)  
nuance.

**[05:31:45.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007150)  
Anong kaibahan ng irata as against amendment at anong implikasyonist yan?

**[05:31:50.940] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007151)  
Ang pagkakaintindi ko po sa aking limited knowledge, dahil first budget po talaga nating sinusumite ito.

**[05:31:59.840] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007152)  
Kapag po irata, yan po ay hindi i-EFLR.

**[05:32:04.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007153)  
Pero pag ito ay amendment, it will take the form of a change in the NEP.

**[05:32:11.460] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007154)  
So, ma-EFLR din po yan.

**[05:32:16.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007155)  
Amendment is a congressional amendment.

**[05:32:18.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007156)  
Opo

**[05:32:19.000] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007157)  
Of course, this is dependent on or subject to the wisdom of the, of Congress.

**[05:32:25.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007158)  
In short, irata, there will be congressional.

**[05:32:29.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007159)  
Well, the usual term for that is insertions.

**[05:32:34.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007160)  
Well, amendments.

**[05:32:36.100] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007161)  
I think yung amendment naman po, I don't think that can be considered an insertion.

**[05:32:40.060] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007162)  
I don't know, no?

**[05:32:40.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007163)  
Sa pagkakaintindi ko po, no?

**[05:32:42.093] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007164)  
Okay.

**[05:32:42.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007165)  
Kasi ito naman po, kasama naman po sa power of the purse.

**[05:32:46.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007166)  
Ang kongreso, yung pagbabago, I think insertion lang po ata siya kapag siya ay post-gap.

**[05:32:54.860] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007167)  
Sa aking pagkakaintindi po, I could be wrong.

**[05:32:56.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007168)  
Okay.

**[05:32:57.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007169)  
Mr. Chairman.

**[05:32:58.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007170)  
So, irata, instead, congressional amendments.

**[05:33:01.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007171)  
And, this will be subject to FLR.

**[05:33:05.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007172)  
Okay.

**[05:33:06.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007173)  
Okay.

**[05:33:07.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007174)

Last question na.

Round three na lang, if ever.

**[05:33:14.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007177)

**[05:33:27.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007184)  
Okay.

**[05:33:28.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007185)  
Balik.

**[05:33:30.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007186)

**[05:33:38.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007189)

Delay.

Next slide po.

**[05:33:57.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007195)

**[05:33:58.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007196)  
For 2026, out of 17,000 national projects, as of August, third quarter, 32% pa lang

**[05:34:15.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007198)  
Delay.

**[05:34:15.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007199)

**[05:34:26.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007201)  
Okay.

**[05:34:27.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007202)

**[05:34:35.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007203)

**[05:34:44.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007204)

**[05:34:47.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-007207)

**[05:35:10.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007212)

**[05:35:11.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007213)

**[05:35:13.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007214)

**[05:35:17.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007215)

**[05:35:24.680] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007216)

**[05:35:30.600] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007217)

**[05:35:34.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007218)

**[05:35:37.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007219)

**[05:35:39.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007221)

**[05:35:43.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007222)

**[05:35:50.320] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007223)

**[05:35:53.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007224)

**[05:35:55.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007225)

**[05:36:00.260] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007226)

**[05:36:02.620] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007227)

**[05:36:10.960] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007229)

**[05:36:12.900] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007230)

**[05:37:11.400] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007246)

**[05:37:14.340] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007247)

**[05:37:17.140] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007248)

**[05:37:20.820] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007249)

**[05:37:28.180] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007250)  
Kasi hindi naman nabubuo, e.

**[05:37:30.000] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007251)

**[05:37:33.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007252)

**[05:37:38.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007253)

**[05:37:40.360] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007254)

**[05:37:42.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007255)  
Tapos nun, kina-cut na

**[05:37:44.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007256)

**[05:37:47.760] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007257)

**[05:37:50.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007258)

**[05:37:52.140] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007259)

**[05:37:53.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007260)

**[05:37:54.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007261)

**[05:37:58.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007262)  
Tapos nun, wala namang kadugtong.

**[05:38:00.240] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007263)

**[05:38:03.280] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007264)  
Tama po kayo, Congressman Bong.

**[05:38:07.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007265)  
Ideal po talaga.

**[05:38:08.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007266)  
Dapat ganon.

**[05:38:09.400] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007267)

**[05:38:12.540] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007268)

**[05:38:15.380] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007269)

**[05:38:17.740] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007270)

**[05:38:25.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007272)

**[05:38:29.420] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007273)

**[05:38:38.480] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007276)

**[05:38:41.180] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007277)

**[05:38:43.040] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007278)

**[05:38:52.500] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007280)

**[05:38:56.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007281)

**[05:38:57.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007282)

**[05:39:01.360] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007283)  
Pero kailangan po talaga mag-focus

**[05:39:03.140] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007284)

**[05:39:05.640] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007285)

**[05:39:09.520] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007286)

**[05:39:11.780] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007287)  
Dahil po,

**[05:39:12.820] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007288)

**[05:39:18.020] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007289)

**[05:39:19.700] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007290)  
Pero, ano po, agree po ako at baka po matulungan nyo kami

**[05:39:28.580] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007292)

**[05:39:31.220] DPWH Secretary Vivencio B. Dizon** (asr-007293)

**[05:39:35.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007294)  
And, well, I look forward to that day na

**[05:39:38.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007295)

**[05:39:42.120] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007296)  
Pero, kagaya ng sinabi nyo,

**[05:39:43.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007297)

**[05:39:45.780] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007298)

**[05:39:49.580] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007299)

**[05:39:52.740] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007300)  
So, for example, a good example na lang.

**[05:39:55.700] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007301)  
Napakaikli ng San Juan River na dumadaan sa distrito ko

**[05:40:00.500] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007302)

**[05:40:04.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007303)

**[05:40:06.620] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007304)

**[05:40:11.140] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007305)  
So, ganun din.

**[05:40:12.860] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007306)  
And, sa iba, pag lumipad ka over Bulacan,

**[05:40:15.140] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007307)

**[05:40:17.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-007308)
